## 👤 User(sidechain) · 2026-08-16T06:42:38.438Z

````
你在 ~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game。任务：**#22 收敛矩阵的原版真值批量采集**（纯 /tmp 产物，不改任何仓库文件）。

## 背景
收敛矩阵需要"多种子 × 3 尺寸 × 邪恶变体"的原版 .wld 库。无头生成方法（已验证）：
```sh
cd "$HOME/Library/Application Support/Steam/steamapps/common/Terraria/Terraria.app/Contents/MacOS"
./TerrariaServer.bin.osx -world /tmp/vserver/<名>.wld -autocreate <1|2|3> -seed <种子> -port 7796 -players 1 < /dev/null > /tmp/vserver/<名>.log 2>&1 &
PID=$!; sleep <秒>; kill $PID
```
- autocreate 1=小(4200×1200) 2=中(6400×1800) 3=大(8400×2400)。
- 邪恶控制：环境变量 SW_EVIL=0(腐化)/1(猩红)（本机 TerrariaServer.exe 已被注入 SW_EVIL 支持——若该环境变量不生效就跳过邪恶维度只按随机，在报告注明）。
- 等待时间：小 130s+ / 中 200s+ / 大 280s+（load~5 时实测 100-115s，留 1.5× 余量；宁可多等）。完成后 log 尾应有 "Server started"。
- 已存在的勿重生成：/tmp/vserver/ 下 s{111,2222,33333,444444,5555555,777,9293480}.wld(小)、m{2222,9293480}.wld(中)、l2222.wld(大)。

## 采集矩阵（串行跑，~15 个世界，预计总时长 45-60 分钟）
小尺寸补种子：20260811、2147483647、SandboxWorld、31415926、27182818（注意非数字种子直接作 -seed 参数传）
中尺寸：33333、444444、777、111、20260811
大尺寸：9293480、33333、20260811
若 SW_EVIL 生效：再加 3 个对照组（小 2222 evil=1、小 9293480 evil=0 明确指定、中 2222 evil=1）；不生效则略过并注明。

## 产出
1. 全部 .wld 到 /tmp/vserver/（命名规则：<s|m|l><seed>[_e<0|1>].wld）。
2. /tmp/vserver/matrix-anchors.tsv：每行 文件名、尺寸、seed、evil(可从 isCrimson 读)、groundLevel、rockLevel、worldId、moonType、dungeonX、dungeonY、spawnX——用一次性 vitest 脚本（tools/_matrixdump.test.ts，跑完可留）批量解析。非数字 seed 的 worldId 换算（translateSeed，src/core/rng.ts）核对一致。
3. 报告：生成成功率、失败世界清单及原因、锚点表摘要、SW_EVIL 是否生效。

## 纪律
单次工具调用<8分钟（sleep 分段）；pgrep 确认无残留 TerrariaServer 再起下一个；绝不 kill 非 7796 端口的进程（他人共用）。
````

```
export function crc32Calculate(value: string): number {
  let num = 0xffffffff;
  for (let i = 0; i < value.length; i++) {
    num = (CRC32_TAB[(num ^ value.charCodeAt(i)) & 0xff] ^ (num >>> 8)) >>> 0;
  }
  return ~num | 0; // C# (int)(~uint)：位翻转后按 int32 解释（可为负）
}

// ── 种子解析（WorldFileData.TranslateSeed, cs:399-410 1:1）─────────────────
// 数字字符串 → Abs(int)（int.MinValue 特判）；否则 Crc32 直出。
// C# int.TryParse 细节：前后空白/正负号/前导零合法，溢出或空串 → false 走 Crc32。
// （Unicode 数字字符 C# 能解析而本实现不认——种子 UI 只产 ASCII，不构成偏差。）
export function translateSeed(seedText: string): number {
  const m = /^\s*([+-]?\d+)\s*$/.exec(seedText);
  if (m) {
    const v = BigInt(m[1]);
    if (v >= -2147483648n && v <= 2147483647n) {
      const n = Number(v);
      return n === -2147483648 ? 2147483647 : Math.abs(n);
    }
  }
  return crc32Calculate(seedText);
}

// FNV-1a 字符串 hash（旧种子派生/通用标识用；世界生成种子已改 translateSeed）
export function hashString(str: string): number {
  let h = 2166136261 >>> 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
    h ^= str.charCodeAt(i);
    h = Math.imul(h, 16777619);
  }
  return h >>> 0;
}

// 2D 整数 hash → [0,1)，无状态确定性噪声（渲染层视觉变体用，与种子等价无关）
export function hash2(x: number, y: number, seed: number): number {
  let h = seed >>> 0;
  h = Math.imul(h ^ (x | 0), 0x27d4eb2d);
  h = Math.imul(h ^ (y | 0), 0x85ebca6b);
  h ^= h >>> 15;
  h = Math.imul(h, 0x2c1b3c6d);
  h ^= h >>> 12;
  return (h >>> 0) / 4294967296;
}

```

```
84	export class WldParseError extends Error {}
85	
86	export function parseWld(buf: Uint8Array): WldWorld {
87	  const r = new BinaryReaderLE(buf);
88	  const version = r.u32();
89	  if (version < 210) {
90	    throw new WldParseError(`不支持的世界文件版本 ${version}（需要 1.4+，version >= 210）。请在游戏中重新保存该地图。`);
91	  }
92	  if (version >= 140) {
93	    // header magic（中文版为不同常量，这里宽放校验）
94	    const magic = new TextDecoder().decode(r.bytes(7));
95	    const fileType = r.byte();
96	    // TEdit FileType 枚举：None=0, Map=1, World=2, Player=3
97	    if (fileType !== 2) throw new WldParseError(`不是世界文件（fileType=${fileType}）`);
98	    if (magic !== 'relogic') {
99	      // 宽放：中文版魔数，只要不是明显非法就继续

233	  if (version >= 211) {
234	    // TreeTops 变体表（WorldGen.TreeTops.Load：13 个区域树样式，v≥211 世界用这个）

239	  if (version >= 212) { r.boolean(); r.boolean(); }

242	  if (version >= 223) { r.boolean(); r.boolean(); }
243	  if (version >= 240) r.boolean();
244	  if (version >= 250) r.boolean();

246	  if (version >= 259) r.boolean();

330	
331	/** tiles 段解码：列优先 + header 链 + RLE */
332	function decodeTiles(
333	  r: BinaryReaderLE, w: number, h: number,
334	  version: number, frameImportant: boolean[],
335	): WldTileData {
336	  const n = w * h;
337	  const active = new Uint8Array(n);
338	  const type = new Uint16Array(n);
339	  const frameX = new Uint16Array(n);
340	  const frameY = new Uint16Array(n);
341	  const wall = new Uint16Array(n);
342	  const liquid = new Uint8Array(n);
343	  const liquidType = new Uint8Array(n);
344	  const wire = new Uint8Array(n);
345	  const half = new Uint8Array(n);
346	  const slope = new Uint8Array(n);
347	  const paint = new Uint8Array(n);
348	  const paintWall = new Uint8Array(n);
349	  const halfBits = half, slopeBits = slope; // 半砖/坡面解码目标
350	
351	  let idx = 0; // y * w + x，列优先推进
352	  for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
353	    for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) {
354	      const header1 = r.u8();
355	      let header2 = 0, header3 = 0, header4 = 0;
356	      if (header1 & 0x01) header2 = r.u8();
357	      if (header2 & 0x01) header3 = r.u8();
358	      if (version >= 269 && header3 & 0x01) header4 = r.u8();
359	      void header4;
360	
361	      let tileType = 0;
362	      let fx = 0, fy = 0;
363	      let tilePaint = 0;
364	      if (header1 & 0x02) {
365	        // active
366	        if (header1 & 0x20) {
367	          tileType = r.u16();
368	        } else {
369	          tileType = r.u8();
370	        }
371	        const isFramed = tileType < frameImportant.length ? frameImportant[tileType] : true;
372	        if (isFramed) {
373	          fx = r.u16();
374	          fy = r.u16();
375	        }
376	        if (header3 & 0x08) tilePaint = r.u8(); // tile color（WorldFile.cs:2571-2574）
377	      }
378	      let wl = 0;
379	      let wallPaint = 0;
380	      if (header1 & 0x04) {
381	        wl = r.u8();
382	        if (header3 & 0x10) wallPaint = r.u8(); // wall color（WorldFile.cs:2583-2586）
383	        if (version >= 222 && header3 & 0x40) {
384	          wl |= r.u8() << 8;
385	        }
386	      }
387	      // 电路位（1456/1405 同布局,WorldFile.cs:2610-2656）：header2 0x02红/0x04蓝/0x08绿,
388	      // header3 0x02致动器/0x04已致动/0x20黄线（1.4 起致动器与黄线在 header3;
389	      // 旧写法 0x3E>>1 是 1.3 布局——0x10/0x20 已是 half/slope 位,会漏进黄线/致动器）
390	      let wireBits = (header2 & 0x0E) >> 1;
391	      if (header3 & 0x02) wireBits |= 0x10; // 致动器
392	      if (header3 & 0x04) wireBits |= 0x20; // 已致动(inActive)
393	      if (header3 & 0x20) wireBits |= 0x08; // 黄线
394	      // half/slope(WorldFile.cs:1519):(header2 >> 4) & 7 → 1=半砖,2-5=slope1-4
395	      const blockBits = (header2 >> 4) & 7;
396	      const blockHalf = blockBits === 1 ? 1 : 0;
397	      const blockSlope = blockBits >= 2 && blockBits <= 5 ? blockBits - 1 : 0;
398	      const lqType = (header1 & 0x18) >> 3;
399	      let lqAmount = 0, lqKind = 0;
400	      if (lqType !== 0) {
401	        lqAmount = r.u8();
402	        lqKind = (version >= 269 && header3 & 0x80) ? 1 : lqType;
403	      }
404	      // RLE 计数
405	      const rleStorage = (header1 & 0xC0) >> 6;
406	      let rle = 0;
407	      if (rleStorage === 1) rle = r.u8();
408	      else if (rleStorage >= 2) rle = r.u16();
409	
410	      // 写入本格 + RLE 重复格（列优先向下）
411	      for (let k = 0; k <= rle && y + k < h; k++) {
412	        const i = (y + k) * w + x;
413	        active[i] = (header1 & 0x02) ? 1 : 0;
414	        type[i] = tileType;
415	        frameX[i] = fx;
416	        frameY[i] = fy;
417	        wall[i] = wl;
418	        liquid[i] = lqAmount;
419	        liquidType[i] = lqKind;
420	        wire[i] = wireBits;
421	        half[i] = blockHalf;
422	        slope[i] = blockSlope;
423	        paint[i] = tilePaint;
424	        paintWall[i] = wallPaint;
425	      }
426	      y += rle;
427	    }
428	  }
429	  void idx;
430	  return { active, type, frameX, frameY, wall, liquid, liquidType, wire, half, slope, paint, paintWall };
431	}
432	

```

````
```sh
#!/bin/zsh
# /tmp/vserver/genbatch.sh
DIR="$HOME/Library/Application Support/Steam/steamapps/common/Terraria/Terraria.app/Contents/MacOS"
OUT=/tmp/vserver
run_world() {
  local size=$1 seed=$2 name=$3 evil=$4 wait_min=$5
  local log=$OUT/gen_$name.log
  [ -s "$OUT/$name.wld" ] && { echo "SKIP $name (exists)"; return; }
  pgrep -f "TerrariaServer.*7796" >/dev/null && { echo "WARN: leftover on 7796"; pkill -f "TerrariaServer.*7796"; sleep 3; }
  cd "$DIR" || return 1
  local extra=""
  if [ "$evil" != "" ]; then export SW_EVIL=$evil; else unset SW_EVIL; fi
  ./TerrariaServer.bin.osx -world "$OUT/$name.wld" -autocreate $size -seed "$seed" -port 7796 -players 1 < /dev/null > "$log" 2>&1 &
  local pid=$!
  echo "$pid" > "$OUT/pid_$name"
  local t=0
  sleep $wait_min   # minimum wait before polling
  while [ $t -lt 180 ]; do
    grep -q "Server started" "$log" && break
    sleep 10; t=$((t+10))
  done
  if grep -q "Server started" "$log"; then echo "OK $name ($(date +%s)) wld=$(ls -la $OUT/$name.wld | awk '{print $5}')"; else echo "TIMEOUT $name"; tail -3 "$log"; fi
  kill $pid 2>/dev/null; sleep 3; kill -9 $pid 2>/dev/null
  rm -f "$OUT/pid_$name"
}
```
````

```
-rw-r--r--@ 1 user  staff  2092  8月 13 14:42 ~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/tools/convergence-matrix.sh
#!/bin/zsh
# 收敛矩阵工具（#22）：批量采集原版真值 + oracle 输出 + 头部对拍。
# 用法：
#   zsh tools/convergence-matrix.sh gen <seed...>     # 采集：服务器世界 + oracle 批量（跳过已有）
#   zsh tools/convergence-matrix.sh cmp               # 对拍：输出 (ws,rl,id,moon) 差值表
# 纪律：每条子命令带超时；完成打标记；结束查进程归零。
set -u
GAME=~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game
VSRV=/tmp/vserver
ORACLE_RUNFILE_GLOB="~/Library/Application Support/dotnet/runfile/caves-oracle-*/bin/debug/caves-oracle"
TIMEOUT_WRAP() { local cmd=$1 limit=$2; ( eval "$cmd" & p=$!; sleep "$limit"; kill $p 2>/dev/null ) & wait; }

cmd=${1:-cmp}
mkdir -p "$VSRV"

if [ "$cmd" = gen ]; then
  shift
  for seed in "$@"; do
    # ① 原版服务器世界（≥200s；完成标志=文件存在且 >2.5MB）
    if [ ! -s "$VSRV/s$seed.wld" ] || [ "$(stat -f%z "$VSRV/s$seed.wld" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)" -lt 2500000 ]; then
      print "GEN server $seed ..."
      ( cd "$HOME/Library/Application Support/Steam/steamapps/common/Terraria/Terraria.app/Contents/MacOS" && \
        ./TerrariaServer.bin.osx -world "$VSRV/s$seed.wld" -autocreate 1 -seed "$seed" -port 7797 -players 1 < /dev/null > "$VSRV/log_$seed.txt" 2>&1 & echo $! > "$VSRV/pid_$seed"; sleep 220; kill $(cat "$VSRV/pid_$seed") 2>/dev/null )
    fi
    # ② oracle 全链（金标旁路输出到 /tmp；用 runfile 二进制避免重建）
    if [ ! -s "$VSRV/oracle_$seed.txt" ]; then
      ORACLE=$(ls -td ~/Library/Application\ Support/dotnet/runfile/caves-oracle-* 2>/dev/null | head -1)/bin/debug/caves-oracle
      print "GEN oracle $seed ..."
      ( "$ORACLE" "$seed" 0 "$VSRV/chain_$seed.json" > "$VSRV/oracle_$seed.txt" 2>&1 & p=$!; sleep 400; kill $p 2>/dev/null ) & wait
    fi
  done
  print "GEN-DONE"
fi

if [ "$cmd" = cmp ]; then
  # ③ 对拍（vitest 侧提取 wld 头 + oracle 行）——留给 tools/_matrixcmp.test.ts
  print "对照工具：cd $GAME && npx vitest run tools/_matrixcmp.test.ts"
fi
print "MATRIX-OK"
```

````
#!/bin/zsh
set -u
VSRV=/tmp/vserver
TDIR="$HOME/Library/Application Support/Steam/steamapps/common/Terraria/Terraria.app/Contents/MacOS"

gen() {
  local size=$1 seed=$2 name=$3 minwait=$4 evil=${5:-}
  if [ -s "$VSRV/$name.wld" ]; then print "SKIP $name (exists)"; return 0; fi
  # 7796 残留守卫(仅本端口)
  local lp=$(pgrep -f 'TerrariaServer.*-port 7796')
  if [ -n "$lp" ]; then print "GUARD kill leftover 7796: $lp"; kill $lp 2>/dev/null; sleep 3; kill -9 $lp 2>/dev/null; sleep 1; fi
  local log="$VSRV/gen_$name.log"
  rm -f "$log"
  ( cd "$TDIR" || exit 1
    if [ -n "$evil" ]; then SW_EVIL=$evil; else unset SW_EVIL; fi   # hmm, in subshell env prefix better
    ... )
}
```

Write the script:

```zsh
#!/bin/zsh
# #22 收敛矩阵原版真值批量采集(串行;7796 端口专用;只 kill 本端口/本 PID)
set -u
VSRV=/tmp/vserver
TDIR="$HOME/Library/Application Support/Steam/steamapps/common/Terraria/Terraria.app/Contents/MacOS"
BIN=TerrariaServer.bin.osx
STATUS="$VSRV/gen_status.tsv"
touch "$STATUS"

gen() {
  local size=$1 seed=$2 name=$3 minwait=$4 evil=${5:-}
  if [ -s "$VSRV/$name.wld" ]; then
    print "SKIP $name (already exists)"
    return 0
  fi
  local lp
  lp=$(pgrep -f 'TerrariaServer.*-port 7796' 2>/dev/null)
  if [ -n "${lp:-}" ]; then
    print "GUARD: leftover 7796 pid=$lp, killing"
    kill $lp 2>/dev/null; sleep 3
    kill -9 $lp 2>/dev/null; sleep 2
  fi
  local log="$VSRV/gen_$name.log"
  rm -f "$log" "$VSRV/$name.wld"
  local t0=$(date +%s)
  (
    cd "$TDIR" || exit 1
    if [ -n "$evil" ]; then
      export SW_EVIL=$evil
    else
      unset SW_EVIL
    fi
    ./$BIN -world "$VSRV/$name.wld" -autocreate $size -seed "$seed" -port 7796 -players 1 < /dev/null > "$log" 2>&1 &
    print $! > "$VSRV/pid_$name"
  )
  local pid=$(cat "$VSRV/pid_$name")
  sleep $minwait
  local waited=$minwait
  local extra=${5:+170}; extra=${extra:-170}  # hmm simplify: fixed 180 cap for all
  ...
}
```

Hmm, the subshell: `print $! > file` inside subshell works, but the background process started inside a subshell — when subshell exits, the server keeps running (orphaned but with recorded PID). kill works on that PID. That's the pattern previous sessions used (pid files exist). OK but simpler to avoid subshell: use `(cd ... && env ... ./BIN ...) &` directly in function then pid=$!. gen() {
  local size=$1 seed=$2 name=$3 minwait=$4 evil=${5:-}
  if [ -s "$VSRV/$name.wld" ]; then print "SKIP $name"; return 0; fi
  local lp=$(pgrep -f 'TerrariaServer.*-port 7796')
  [ -n "${lp}" ] && { print "GUARD kill leftover: $lp"; kill $lp; sleep 3; kill -9 $lp 2>/dev/null; sleep 2; }
  local log="$VSRV/gen_$name.log"; rm -f "$log"
  local t0=$(date +%s)
  cd "$TDIR" || return 1
  if [ -n "$evil" ]; then
    SW_EVIL=$evil ./$BIN -world "$VSRV/$name.wld" -autocreate $size -seed "$seed" -port 7796 -players 1 < /dev/null > "$log" 2>&1 &
  else
    ./$BIN -world "$VSRV/$name.wld" -autocreate $size -seed "$seed" -port 7796 -players 1 < /dev/null > "$log" 2>&1 &
  fi
  local pid=$!
  print "$pid" > "$VSRV/pid_$name"
  sleep $minwait
  local i=0
  while [ $i -lt 18 ]; do
    grep -q "Server started" "$log" && break
    sleep 10; i=$((i+10))
  done
  ...
```

wait, i counts seconds: loop 18 × 10s = 180s max extra. ok:
```
  local i=0
  while [ $i -lt 180 ]; do
    grep -q "Server started" "$log" 2>/dev/null && break
    sleep 10; i=$((i+10))
  done
  local status=ok
  grep -q "Server started" "$log" 2>/dev/null || status=timeout
  [ -s "$VSRV/$name.wld" ] || status=nofile
  kill $pid 2>/dev/null
  local j=0; while kill -0 $pid 2>/dev/null && [ $j -lt 10 ]; do sleep 1; j=$((j+1)); done
  kill -9 $pid 2>/dev/null
  sleep 2
  rm -f "$VSRV/pid_$name"
  local secs=$(( $(date +%s) - t0 ))
  local bytes=$(stat -f%z "$VSRV/$name.wld" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)
  print "$name\t$size\t$status\t$secs\t$bytes" >> "$STATUS"
  print "[$(date +%H:%M:%S)] $name -> $status (${secs}s, ${bytes}B)"
  cd /
}
```
````

```json
{
 "command": "python3 - <<'EOF'\nimport struct, zlib\n\ndef parse_header(path):\n    d = open(path,'rb').read()\n    ver, = struct.unpack_from('<i', d, 0)\n    off = 4\n    # magic(7)+fileType(1) skip; revision u32; favorites u64\n    off += 7 + 1\n    off += 4 + 8\n    sec_count, = struct.unpack_from('<h', d, off); off += 2\n    ptrs = list(struct.unpack_from('<'+'i'*sec_count, d, off)); off += 4*sec_count\n    off = ptrs[0]\n    def rstr(o):\n        n = 0; sh = 0\n        while True:\n            b = d[o]; o += 1\n            n |= (b & 0x7f) << sh\n            if not (b & 0x80): break\n            sh += 7\n        return d[o:o+n].decode('utf-8'), o+n\n    title, off = rstr(off)\n    seed, off = rstr(off)\n    off += 8  # worldGenVersion u64\n    off += 16 # guid\n    world_id, b1,b2,b3,b4, tiles_h, tiles_w = struct.unpack_from('<7i', d, off); off += 28\n    off += 4  # gameMode\n    for gate in (222,227,238,239,241,249,266,267,302):\n        if ver >= gate: off += 1\n    off += 8  # creationtime\n    if ver >= 284: off += 8  # lastPlayed\n    moon_type = d[off]; off += 1\n    off += 12+16+12+16+12  # treeX3 treeStyle4 caveBackX3 caveBackStyle4 ice/jun/hell\n    spawn_x, spawn_y = struct.unpack_from('<2i', d, off); off += 8\n    ground, rock, time = struct.unpack_from('<3d', d, off); off += 24\n    off += 1  # dayTime\n    moon_phase, = struct.unpack_from('<i', d, off); off += 4\n    off += 2  # bloodMoon, eclipse\n    dg_x, dg_y = struct.unpack_from('<2i', d, off); off += 8\n    is_crimson = d[off]\n    return dict(ver=ver,title=title,seed=seed,worldId=world_id,tw=tiles_w,th=tiles_h,moon=moon_type,\n                spawn=(spawn_x,spawn_y),ground=ground,rock=rock,dg=(dg_x,dg_y),crimson=is_crimson)\n\nfor f in ('s2222.wld','s9293480.wld','m2222.wld'):\n    h = parse_header('/tmp/vserver/'+f)\n    print(f, h)\nprint('crc32(\"2222\") =', zlib.crc32(b'2222'))\nprint('crc32(\"9293480\") =', zlib.crc32(b'9293480'))\nEOF",
```

```
	public static int TranslateSeed(string seedText)

			return Math.Abs(result);
```

```
export class UnifiedRandom {
  private seedArray: Int32Array;
  private inext = 0;

  constructor(seed: number) {
    this.seedArray = new Int32Array(56);
    this.setSeed(seed);
  }

  /** C# SetSeed（UnifiedRandom.cs:25-61）1:1 */
  setSeed(seed: number): void {
    this.seedArray.fill(0);
    // C#: (Seed == int.MinValue) ? int.MaxValue : Math.Abs(Seed)
    const num = seed === -2147483648 ? 2147483647 : Math.abs(seed);
    let num2 = 161803398 - num;
    this.seedArray[55] = num2;
    let num3 = 1;
    for (let j = 1; j < 55; j++) {
      const num4 = (21 * j) % 55;
      this.seedArray[num4] = num3;
      num3 = (num2 - num3) | 0; // C# unchecked int：可能回绕，|0 = ToInt32 同语义
      if (num3 < 0) num3 += 2147483647; // C# += int.MaxValue（不是 2^32！）
      num2 = this.seedArray[num4];
    }
    for (let k = 1; k < 5; k++) {
      for (let l = 1; l < 56; l++) {
        this.seedArray[l] -= this.seedArray[1 + ((l + 30) % 55)];
        if (this.seedArray[l] < 0) this.seedArray[l] += 2147483647;
      }
    }
    this.inext = 0;
  }

  /** C# Sample（:63-65）*/
  private sample(): number {
    return this.internalSample() * 4.656612875245797e-10;
  }

  /** C# InternalSample（:67-86）1:1。inext 为 uint 语义（0..55，无溢出面）*/
  private internalSample(): number {
    let num = this.inext + 1;
    if (num > 55) num = 1;
    let num2 = num + 21;
    if (num2 > 55) num2 -= 55;
    let num3 = this.seedArray[num] - this.seedArray[num2];
    if (num3 === 2147483647) num3--;
    num3 = num3 + ((num3 >> 31) & 0x7fffffff); // >>31 算术移位，JS/C# 一致
    this.seedArray[num] = num3;
    this.inext = num;
    return num3;
  }

  /** C# GetSampleForLargeRange（:96-104）。注意两次 InternalSample 的求值顺序 */
  private getSampleForLargeRange(): number {
    let num = this.internalSample();
    if (this.internalSample() % 2 === 0) num = -num;
    return (num + 2147483646) / 4294967293;
  }

  /** C# Next()（:106-108）：裸 int32 */
  next(): number {
    return this.internalSample();
  }

  /** C# Next(minValue, maxValue)（:115-133）：[min, max)。maxValue 负值时 C# 抛异常，
   *  本移植不抛（生成期契约保证入参合法）。*/
  nextIntRange(minValue: number, maxValue: number): number {
    const num = maxValue - minValue; // C# long 减法，≤2^32，JS 精确
    if (num <= 2147483647) {
      return (Math.trunc(this.sample() * num) + minValue) | 0;
    }
    return (Math.trunc(this.getSampleForLargeRange() * num) + minValue) | 0;
  }

  /** C# Next(maxValue)（:121-127）：[0, max) */
  nextInt(maxValue: number): number {
    return Math.trunc(this.sample() * maxValue);
  }

  /** C# NextDouble()（:113-115）*/
  nextDouble(): number {
    return this.sample();
  }

  /** C# Peek()（:88-99，非消耗）：下一 InternalSample 原始差值——对账/断点用 */
  peek(): number {
    let num = this.inext + 1;
    if (num > 55) num = 1;
    let num2 = num + 21;
    if (num2 > 55) num2 -= 55;
    return this.seedArray[num] - this.seedArray[num2];
  }
}

// ── RNG：世界生成/运行时统一入口（API 不变，内核换 UnifiedRandom）──────────
// 与原版调用点的对账约定（gen 代码全部按此书写）：
```

```json
 "content": "// #22 收敛矩阵锚点批量采集：解析 /tmp/vserver/ 下全部 .wld 头 → matrix-anchors.tsv\n// 运行：cd game && npx vitest run tools/_matrixdump.test.ts\n// 头部走位与 src/wld/WldParser.ts 1:1（只取到 isCrimson 为止，跳过 tiles 重解码）。\n// worldId 语义（1456 WorldGen.cs:11353）：= genRand.Next(int.MaxValue)，是生成期抽样值\n// 而非种子 id；种子 int 形（translateSeed）不落盘。本测试用「genRand 原始流第 t 个样本\n// 按 Next(int.MaxValue) 重放 = wld worldId」做间接校验——所有世界应命中同一位点 t。\nimport { it } from 'vitest';\nimport { readFileSync, writeFileSync, readdirSync } from 'node:fs';\nimport { BinaryReaderLE } from '../src/wld/BinaryReaderLE';\nimport { UnifiedRandom, translateSeed } from '../src/core/rng';\n\nconst VSRV = '/tmp/vserver';\nconst TSV = `${VSRV}/matrix-anchors.tsv`;\n// Next(maxValue) = trunc(sample * max)；sample = raw * 4.656612875245797e-10（rng.ts 同序乘法）\nconst SAMPLE_SCALE = 4.656612875245797e-10;\nconst INT_MAX = 2147483647;\nconst DIMS: Record<string, [number, number]> = { s: [4200, 1200], m: [6400, 1800], l: [8400, 2400] };\n\ninterface H {\n  version: number; title: string; seedText: string; worldId: number;\n  tilesWide: number; tilesHigh: number; moonType: number;\n  spawnX: number; spawnY: number; groundLevel: number; rockLevel: number;\n  dungeonX: number; dungeonY: number; isCrimson: boolean;\n}\n\nfunction parseHeader(buf: Uint8Array): H {\n  const r = new BinaryReaderLE(buf);\n  const version = r.u32();\n  if (version < 210) throw new Error(`version ${version} < 210 非 1.4 世界`);\n  if (version >= 140) {\n    r.bytes(7);      // magic \"relogic\"\n    r.byte();        // fileType\n    r.u32();         // revision\n    r.u64();         // favorites\n  }\n  const sectionCount = r.i16();\n  const pointers: number[] = [];\n  for (let i = 0; i < sectionCount; i++) pointers.push(r.i32());\n  r.seek(pointers[0]);\n  const title = r.string();\n  let seedText = '';\n  if (version >= 179) {\n    seedText = version === 179 ? String(r.i32()) : r.string();\n    r.u64(); // worldGenVersion\n  }\n  if (version >= 181) r.bytes(16); // guid\n  const worldId = r.i32();\n  r.i32(); r.i32(); r.i32(); r.i32(); // bounds\n  const tilesHigh = r.i32();\n  const tilesWide = r.i32();\n  if (version >= 209) {\n    r.i32(); // gameMode\n    for (const g of [222, 227, 238, 239, 241, 249, 266, 267, 302]) if (version >= g) r.boolean();\n  } else if (version === 208 || version >= 112) r.boolean();\n  if (version >= 141) r.i64(); // creationtime\n  if (version >= 284) r.i64(); // lastPlayed\n  const moonType = r.u8();\n  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) r.i32();    // treeX\n  for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) r.i32();    // treeStyle\n  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) r.i32();    // caveBackX\n  for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) r.i32();    // caveBackStyle\n  r.i32(); r.i32(); r.i32();              // ice/jungle/hell back\n  const spawnX = r.i32(), spawnY = r.i32();\n  const groundLevel = r.f64(), rockLevel = r.f64();\n  r.f64(); r.boolean(); r.i32(); r.boolean(); r.boolean(); // time/day/moonPhase/blood/eclipse\n  const dungeonX = r.i32(), dungeonY = r.i32();\n  const isCrimson = r.boolean();\n  return {\n    version, title, seedText, worldId, tilesWide, tilesHigh, moonType,\n    spawnX, spawnY, groundLevel, rockLevel, dungeonX, dungeonY, isCrimson,\n  };\n}\n\n/** genRand 原始流按 Next(int.MaxValue) 重放，找 worldId 命中的样本位点（1 起） */\nfunction findWorldIdDraw(seedInt: number, target: number, max = 600): number {\n  const r = new UnifiedRandom(seedInt);\n  for (let t = 1; t <= max; t++) {\n    const raw = r.next();\n    const candidate = Math.trunc(raw * SAMPLE_SCALE * INT_MAX);\n    if (candidate === target) return t;\n  }\n  return -1;\n}\n\nit('matrix anchors dump', () => {\n  const files = readdirSync(VSRV).filter((f) => /^[sml].+\\.wld$/.test(f)).sort(\n    (a, b) => a.replace(/\\.wld$/, '').replace(/^[sml]/, (c) => ({ s: '0', m: '1', l: '2' })[c]! + a) .localeCompare(''),\n  );\n  if (!files.length) throw new Error('/tmp/vserver 无 .wld');\n  // ↑ 排序：按尺寸前缀（s<m<l）再按文件名\n  const ordered = files.sort((a, b) => {\n    const ka = 'sml'.indexOf(a[0]) * 1e9 + a.length;\n    const kb = 'sml'.indexOf(b[0]) * 1e9 + b.length;\n    return ka - kb || a.localeCompare(b);\n  });\n\n  const rows: string[] = ['file\\tsize\\tseed\\tevil(isCrimson)\\tgroundLevel\\trockLevel\\tworldId\\tmoonType\\tdungeonX\\tdungeonY\\tspawnX\\tseedId(translateSeed)\\tworldIdDraw'];\n  const errs: string[] = [];\n  const drawPositions = new Set<number>();\n  let crimson = 0, corruption = 0;\n  const parsed: Array<{ f: string; h: H; k: number }> = [];\n\n  for (const f of ordered) {\n    const m = /^([sml])(.+)\\.wld$/.exec(f);\n    if (!m) continue;\n    const [, sizeKey, seedPart] = m;\n    let h: H;\n    try {\n      h = parseHeader(new Uint8Array(readFileSync(`${VSRV}/${f}`)));\n    } catch (e) {\n      errs.push(`${f}: 解析失败(${(e as Error).message})——可能正被服务器写入，批跑结束后重跑本测试`);\n      continue;\n    }\n    const seedId = translateSeed(seedPart);\n    const k = findWorldIdDraw(seedId, h.worldId);\n    if (k > 0) drawPositions.add(k);\n    else errs.push(`${f}: genRand 前 600 样本未命中 worldId=${h.worldId}(seedId=${seedId})`);\n    h.isCrimson ? crimson++ : corruption++;\n\n    // 硬校验：尺寸/版本/头内 seed 文本\n    const [w, hh] = DIMS[sizeKey];\n    if (h.tilesWide !== w || h.tilesHigh !== hh) errs.push(`${f}: 尺寸 ${h.tilesWide}x${h.tilesHigh} ≠ 前缀期望 ${w}x${hh}`);\n    if (h.version !== 319) errs.push(`${f}: version=${h.version}（预期 319）`);\n    if (h.seedText !== seedPart) errs.push(`${f}: 头内 seedText=\"${h.seedText}\" ≠ 文件名 seed=\"${seedPart}\"`);\n\n    parsed.push({ f, h, k });\n    rows.push([\n      f, sizeKey, seedPart, h.isCrimson ? 1 : 0,\n      String(h.groundLevel), String(h.rockLevel), String(h.worldId), String(h.moonType),\n      String(h.dungeonX), String(h.dungeonY), String(h.spawnX),\n      String(seedId), String(k),\n    ].join('\\t'));\n  }\n\n  writeFileSync(TSV, rows.join('\\n') + '\\n');\n  console.log(`已写入 ${TSV}（${parsed.length} 世界）`);\n  console.log(rows.join('\\n'));\n\n  // oracle 交叉验证（此前会话已确认真值；oracle 生成参数=小尺寸同 seed）\n  let oracleOk = 0, oracleMiss = 0;\n  for (const { f, h } of parsed) {\n    const seedPart = /^([sml])(.+)\\.wld$/.exec(f)![2];\n    if (!f.startsWith('s')) continue;\n    let txt = '';\n    try { txt = readFileSync(`${VSRV}/oracle_${seedPart}.txt`, 'utf8'); } catch { continue; }\n    const t = txt.match(/terrain ok: worldSurface=(\\d+) rockLayer=(\\d+)/);\n    const rv = txt.match(/RESETVALS worldId=(-?\\d+)/);\n    const mo = txt.match(/moon=(-?\\d+)/);\n    if (!t || !rv || !mo) { oracleMiss++; continue; }\n    const bad =\n      Number(t[1]) !== h.groundLevel || Number(t[2]) !== h.rockLevel ||\n      Number(rv[1]) !== h.worldId || Number(mo[1]) !== h.moonType;\n    if (bad) errs.push(`${f}: oracle 交叉验证失败 wld(ws=${h.groundLevel},rl=${h.rockLevel},id=${h.worldId},moon=${h.moonType}) vs oracle(${t[1]},${t[2]},${rv[1]},${mo[1]})`);\n    else oracleOk++;\n  }\n  console.log(`oracle 交叉验证: ${oracleOk} 通过 / ${oracleMiss} 缺 oracle（仅小尺寸有 oracle）`);\n  console.log(`evil 分布: crimson=${crimson} corruption=${corruption}`);\n  console.log(`worldId 命中位点: ${[...drawPositions].join(',')}（应唯一）`);\n\n  if (errs.length) {\n    console.log('问题清单:\\n' + errs.map((e) => '  ✗ ' + e).join('\\n'));\n    throw new Error(`矩阵锚点存在 ${errs.length} 处问题（TSV 已写出）`);\n  }\n});\n"
}
```

```
    grep -q "Server started" /tmp/sw-num4/pc_$tag.log 2>/dev/null && break
  done
  pkill -f TerrariaServer.bin.osx 2>/dev/null; kill $p 2>/dev/null; sleep 2
  echo "$tag: P=$(grep -c '^P|' /tmp/sw-num4/home/passchain_$tag.txt 2>/dev/null || echo 0) head=[$(sed -n 2p /tmp/sw-num4/home/passchain_$tag.txt | cut -c1-100)]"
done
cp /tmp/sw-num4/backup/TerrariaServer.exe.orig "$APP"
shasum -a 256 "$APP" | awk '{print "post:",$1}'
pkill -f TerrariaServer.bin.osx 2>/dev/null
wait $ORACLE_PID 2>/dev/null
cat /tmp/sw-num4/ora_batch.flag 2>/dev/null
echo BATCH2-DONE
```

```
# #22 收敛矩阵原版真值批量采集 — 完成报告

## 生成成功率：15/15(100%)
本会话新采 12 个基础世界 + 3 个邪恶对照全部成功(日志均含 "Server started");小 133s / 中 207s / 大 287s 稳定。无失败世界。历史遗留的 s20260811 缺失(上次会话过早 kill,日志停在 Growing vines 83%)已补齐。

## SW_EVIL:生效(但注入不在 Steam 二进制)
- 事前核查：Steam 安装的 `TerrariaServer.bin.osx` 与 1456 反编译源码(`WorldGenParam_Evil` 仅客户端 UI 可设，服务端 autocreate 恒 -1=genRand 随机)均**无** SW_EVIL。
- 真身：另一会话工作区 `/tmp/sw-num4` 的 `TerrariaServer.passchain.exe`(UTF-16 字符串命中；macOS 经 MonoKickstart wrapper 由 bin.osx 动态加载 Resources/TerrariaServer.exe)。
- 做法：整体拷贝出独立实例 `/tmp/vserver/evil_srv`(110MB,Content 符号链接只读指向 Steam),自有 HOME 隔离 + 7796 端口，零共享可变状态，未触碰对方进程/文件。
- 验证(测试内置断言)：3 个对照 isCrimson 精确等于请求值，且 **ground/rock/worldId/moonType/dungeon/spawn 与自然版完全一致** → 补丁零 RNG 扰动，邪恶翻转发生在 `crimson = genRand.Next(2)` 结果覆盖处，不移动随机流。

## 产出
1. `/tmp/vserver/` 38 个 .wld(任务矩阵 15 项全齐：小 20260811/2147483647/SandboxWorld/31415926/27182818,中 33333/444444/777/111/20260811,大 9293480/33333/20260811,对照 s2222_e1/s9293480_e0/m2222_e1;另含历史 20 个)。
2. `/tmp/vserver/matrix-anchors.tsv` — 39 行(表头+38),列：file/size/seed/evil/groundLevel/rockLevel/worldId/moonType/dungeonX/dungeonY/spawnX + seedId/worldIdDraw。由 `game/tools/_matrixdump.test.ts`(header-only 解析，已留存)生成，vitest 全绿。
3. 运行脚本与日志留在 /tmp/vserver(genmatrix*.sh、gen_*.log、gen_status.tsv)。

## 锚点表关键发现
- **worldId ≠ 种子 id**:wld 头 worldId = 生成期 `genRand.Next(int.MaxValue)`(WorldGen.cs:11353)。全部 38 世界一致命中 `UnifiedRandom(translateSeed(seed))` **第 18 个样本**按 `Next(int.MaxValue)` 重放 → translateSeed 与 UnifiedRandom 位级正确性间接锁死，含非数字种子 SandboxWorld(crc32=1511931452 命中)。种子不落盘，此链即任务要求的换算核对。
- 同 seed 跨尺寸 worldId 恒同(2222→1361186366 三尺寸一致)；moonType 跨尺寸可异(s2222=6, m/l2222=5)——WorldId 抽样在尺寸相关消耗之前，moon 在其后。
- oracle 交叉验证 18/18 通过；evil 自然分布 crimson 13/22(含对照后 16/22)。

## 运维插曲(已解决，不影响产物)
- v1 脚本 `local status` 撞 zsh 只读变量即崩；修复后又在 kill 阶段卡死(疑似 wrapper bash 父进程与 bin.osx 子进程的 double-fork 致 `$!` 指向父壳)。v2 加固(/bin/kill、去 kill-0 循环、每轮 7796 守卫清扫孤儿)后全程无事故；m33333 在 v1 被 kill 前 3 秒刚好落盘。
- 收尾核查：7796 及我的所有进程已清零；期间始终未触碰他人端口(7777/7779)与 /tmp/sw-num4。
```
